# SSR Support Card Details

Unique effects, hints and training events. Stat bonuses are in [SSR.md](SSR.md). Source: umamusu.wiki card pages.

## Speed

### [Beyond This Shining Moment] Silence Suzuka

**Unique Effect (Lv1+):** Effect Lv30, Friendship Bonus Increases the effectiveness of Friendship Training +25, Mood Effect Amplifies the effect of mood when training together +30 (+15), Initial Speed Increases initial Speed when beginning a Career playthrough -, Initial Friendship Gauge Increases initial Friendship Gauge when beginning a Career playthrough +20, Race Bonus Increases stat gain from races +5, Fan Bonus Increases fan gain from races +10, Hint Frequency Increases the frequency at which hint events occur (+20), Specialty Priority Increases the frequency at which the character participates in their preferred training type +50, Skill Point Bonus Increases skill point gain when training together -

**Hints**
- Focus: Slightly decrease time lost to slow starts.
- Early Lead: Slightly increase ability to go to the front early-race. (Front Runner)
- Fast-Paced: Slightly increase ability to go to the front mid-race. (Front Runner)
- Final Push: Slightly increase ability to keep the lead on the final corner. (Front Runner)
- Rosy Outlook: Slightly decrease fatigue when positioned toward the front mid-race. (Medium)
- Front Runner Straightaways ○: Slightly increase velocity on a straight. (Front Runner)
- Front Runner Corners ○: Slightly increase velocity on a corner. (Front Runner)
- Front Runner Savvy ○: Moderately increase ability to get into a good position. (Front Runner)
- Leader's Pride: Slightly increase ability to pass or challenge another runner early-race or mid-race. (Front Runner)

### [Dream Big!] Tokai Teio

**Unique Effect (Lv1+):** Effect Lv30, Friendship Bonus Increases the effectiveness of Friendship Training +15 (+10), Mood Effect Amplifies the effect of mood when training together +40, Power Bonus Increases Power gain when training together -, Initial Speed Increases initial Speed when beginning a Career playthrough (+20), Initial Friendship Gauge Increases initial Friendship Gauge when beginning a Career playthrough +20, Race Bonus Increases stat gain from races -, Fan Bonus Increases fan gain from races -, Hint Levels Increases the level of hints gained through events +2, Hint Frequency Increases the frequency at which hint events occur +30, Specialty Priority Increases the frequency at which the character participates in their preferred training type +20

**Hints**
- Prudent Positioning: Increase navigation early-race.
- Nimble Navigator: Slightly increase maneuverability when the way ahead is blocked in the last spurt.
- Go with the Flow: Moderately increase navigation late-race.
- Thunderbolt Step: Moderately avoid becoming surrounded for a medium duration mid-race. (Medium)
- Soft Step: Slightly decrease fatigue when moving sideways. (Medium)
- Shrewd Step: Slightly increase ability to navigate smoothly. (Pace Chaser)

**Hints**

- Gap Closer: Slightly increase spurting ability when positioned toward the back late-race. (Sprint)

- Flustered Front Runners: Slightly increase fatigue for front runners mid-race.

- Tether: Slightly decrease velocity of runners ahead when positioned toward the back late-race. (Medium)

### [ウタエミンナノ] Twin Turbo

**Unique Effect (Lv1+):** Effect Lv30, Friendship Bonus Increases the effectiveness of Friendship Training +15, Mood Effect Amplifies the effect of mood when training together +40, Guts Bonus Increases Guts gain when training together (+2), Initial Guts Increases initial Guts when beginning a Career playthrough -, Initial Friendship Gauge Increases initial Friendship Gauge when beginning a Career playthrough +15, Race Bonus Increases stat gain from races -, Fan Bonus Increases fan gain from races -, Hint Levels Increases the level of hints gained through events +1, Hint Frequency Increases the frequency at which hint events occur +20, Specialty Priority Increases the frequency at which the character participates in their preferred training type +20

**Hints**
- Competitive Spirit ○: Moderately increase performance when many other runners are using the same strategy.
- Ramp Up: Slightly increase velocity when passing another runner mid-race.
- Early Lead: Slightly increase ability to go to the front early-race. (Front Runner)
- Final Push: Slightly increase ability to keep the lead on the final corner. (Front Runner)
- Steadfast: Slightly increase velocity and very minimally increase acceleration when pressured on the final corner or later. (Medium)
- Keeping the Lead: Slightly increase ability to maintain the lead when leading by a fair margin mid-race. (Long)
- Moxie: Slightly reduce fatigue on an uphill. (Front Runner)
- Risky Business: Moderately increase velocity in the second half of the race, but also greatly increase fatigue sometimes.
- Mad Dash: Greatly expend endurance and moderately increase velocity in the early part of mid-race. (Front Runner)
- Confident: Slightly increase Speed and Power when both attributes exceed a certain amount. (Mile/Medium)

### [Trusting the Dice] Nakayama Festa

**Unique Effect (Lv1+):** Effect Lv30, Friendship Bonus Increases the effectiveness of Friendship Training +15, Mood Effect Amplifies the effect of mood when training together +40, Wit Bonus Increases Wit gain when training together +1, Training Effectiveness Increases the effectiveness of training performed together +10, Initial Friendship Gauge Increases initial Friendship Gauge when beginning a Career playthrough +15, Race Bonus Increases stat gain from races -, Fan Bonus Increases fan gain from races -, Hint Levels Increases the level of hints gained through events +2, Hint Frequency Increases the frequency at which hint events occur +40, Skill Point Bonus Increases skill point gain when training together -, Wit Friendship Recovery Increases Energy recovery from Wit Friendship Training +3, Unique: Chance to Set Failure Rate to 0% (+20)

**Hints**
- Wet Conditions ○: Moderately increase performance on good, soft, and heavy ground.
- Summer Runner ○: Moderately increase performance in summer.
- Long Shot ○: Moderately increase performance when 4th favorite or below.
- Nimble Navigator: Slightly increase maneuverability when the way ahead is blocked in the last spurt.
- Slick Surge: Slightly increase acceleration when positioned midpack or further back in the early part of late-race. (Late Surger)
- Steadfast: Slightly increase velocity and very minimally increase acceleration when pressured on the final corner or later. (Medium)
- Late Surger Straightaways ○: Slightly increase velocity on a straight. (Late Surger)
- Lucky Seven: Moderately good things may happen when in bracket 7.
- Triple 7s: Slightly gain energy with 777m remaining.
- Uma Stan: Slightly increase velocity when close to many runners.
- All I've Got: Slightly increase velocity when well-positioned on a straight in the last spurt. (Medium)
- Risky Business: Moderately increase velocity in the second half of the race, but also greatly increase fatigue sometimes.
